# TODO-06 -- TODO Metadata Layer and Derived Graph

> **Validated:** 2026-06-21 | backfill -- todo-graph structural validate clean; all sections shipped + reviewed
> **Gap-audited:** 2026-06-21 | backfill -- triage DONE (sections shipped + quality-reviewed / deferred); Stages 1-2 predate this marker

> **Goal:** Keep markdown TODO files canonical, but add a stable-ID frontmatter layer + a derived index (JSON cache) so cross-TODO dependencies, backlinks, and "ready / blocked / orphaned" queries work without line-number fragility or manual grep sweeps. Markdown stays git-friendly and editor-native; the cache is a read-only projection that the generator rebuilds from the source files.

> [!IMPORTANT]
> **Current state:** The repo has ~86 TODO files under `todo/` organized into 16 domains. Cross-TODO references use a mix of `TODO-NN §N` path refs and a compact `TNN §N` / `DNN TNN §N` shorthand. Semantic identity lives in the filename ("TODO-02-ai-development-system.md"); renumbering or renaming a TODO breaks every inbound XREF silently. The `Deferred:` / `Accepted:` stamp grammar relies on `(item: "NAME" at line N)` parentheticals -- line numbers volatile, stamps decay. `validate-todo-file` and `gap-audit-todo` skills walk the graph in prose at audit time but nothing caches the relationships. The pre-commit hook (`.claude/settings.json` hook 5) blocks bare XREFs but cannot detect broken ones across files. Two incidents in April 2026: TODO-02 §1 stamp needed post-commit correction ("false README link claim"), and §3 Deferred line shipped pointing at line 142 when the real item was at line 127.

## Inputs

- [`todo/`](../../todo/) -- every `.md` file under this tree is a TODO and becomes a node in the graph
- [`todo/TODO-00-INDEX.md`](../TODO-00-INDEX.md) -- root roadmap index; generator reads it to cross-check domain ordering
- [`todo/*/INDEX.md`](../) -- per-domain indexes; each TODO must appear in exactly one
- [`scripts/`](../../scripts/) -- host tooling home for the new generator/validator/query scripts
- [`.github/workflows/build.yml`](../../.github/workflows/build.yml) -- CI hook goes here (§6)
- [`.claude/skills/validate-todo-file/SKILL.md`](../../.claude/skills/validate-todo-file/SKILL.md) -- existing structural validator; §3 extends it with graph checks it cannot do alone
- [`.claude/skills/gap-audit-todo/SKILL.md`](../../.claude/skills/gap-audit-todo/SKILL.md) -- existing graph walker in prose; §4 gives it a machine-readable backend
- [`.claude/settings.json`](../../.claude/settings.json) -- hook 5 blocks bare Accepted/Deferred XREFs at commit; §3 validator extends the check to cross-file XREF integrity, §6 adds a sibling PostToolUse hook for stale-line-number auto-rewrite
- [`docs/infrastructure/ai-system.md`](../../docs/infrastructure/ai-system.md) -- architectural parallel: settings.json canonical, Hook Routing Matrix derived. §1 applies the same invariant to TODO files.
- [`docs/infrastructure/todo-metadata.md`](../../docs/infrastructure/todo-metadata.md) -- new doc shipped by §1 (frontmatter spec for humans)
- [`docs/infrastructure/todo-metadata.schema.json`](../../docs/infrastructure/todo-metadata.schema.json) -- new JSON Schema Draft 2020-12 sidecar shipped by §1 (machine-readable spec for editors + CI lint)
- [`docs/infrastructure/todo-graph.md`](../../docs/infrastructure/todo-graph.md) -- new mermaid-flowchart artifact regenerated by §7 (`make todo-graph-render-mermaid`); rendered inline on GitHub for one-click graph navigation
- -> XREF: [`00-infrastructure/TODO-01 §7 Tooling Doctor and Regression Pack`](TODO-01-developer-tooling-stack.md#7-tooling-doctor-and-regression-pack) -- host-side tooling regression pattern to mirror for §3 validator
- -> XREF: [`00-infrastructure/TODO-02 §9 AI Workflow Regression Suite`](TODO-02-ai-development-system.md#9-ai-workflow-regression-suite) -- complement (checks doctrine presence; this TODO checks cross-TODO graph integrity). Both ship as part of a repo-wide TODO/AI regression story.
- -> XREF: [`00-infrastructure/TODO-07 §1, §7`](TODO-07-lsp-mcp-bridge.md#1-bridge-skeleton--lsp-json-rpc-client) -- LSP-MCP bridge reuses this TODO's §8 FastMCP + stdio + `_CALL_LOCK` + typed-handler pattern. Any change to the shared pattern (e.g. FastMCP API migration, manifest shape change in `.claude/mcp.json`) must land in both TODOs in lockstep.

## Outcome

- Stable semantic ID in every TODO file's YAML frontmatter: `schema_version`, `id`, `domain`, `status`, `title`, optional `$schema` / `owners` / `depends_on` / `satisfies` / `superseded_by` / `file_patterns`. Auto-derived `created_at` / `last_active_at` are CACHE-ONLY (never in frontmatter). File renumbering / renaming stops breaking inbound XREFs.
- JSON Schema sidecar at `docs/infrastructure/todo-metadata.schema.json` (Draft 2020-12) is the canonical authority programmatic validators (generator + CI lint + ad-hoc `jsonschema.validate` calls) follow via the per-file `$schema:` field. The stock Red Hat YAML LS does NOT auto-detect this in markdown frontmatter; editor-time diagnostics require `remark-lint-frontmatter-schema` or `markdown-yaml-embedded-langservers`. The §6 CI gate is the authoritative line of defense.
- `scripts/todo-graph/build.py` generator: parses every `todo/**/*.md`, extracts frontmatter + Implementation Order rows + Accepted/Deferred XREFs + Inputs XREFs, computes git-derived timestamps, emits `build/todo-cache.json` (derived, gitignored). Fast (under 2s for ~86 files). Documented YAML error catalog (5 categories).
- `scripts/todo-graph/validate.py` validator: seven checks -- stale XREFs, dangling section refs, orphaned Implementation-Order rows, dependency cycles, test-runner bat alignment, status-transition audit (`status: done` requires every row `[x]`), `$schema` reachability. Same `t_pass` / `t_fail` pattern as `scripts/test-tooling.sh`. `--diff <baseline>` mode for PR comparisons.
- `scripts/todo-graph/query.py` CLI: 16 subcommands -- the file-level twelve (`ready` / `blocked` / `blocking` / `by-domain` / `backlinks` / `deferred` / `deferred-by` / `orphans` / `stale` / `stats` / `code` / `code-by`), `render`, and the section-level readiness trio (`section-ready` / `section-blocked` / `section-blocking`). 15 of them are mirrored as MCP tools; `render` is deliberately not. Universal `--format markdown` + `--watch` mode.
- `scripts/todo-graph/query.py render` (or sibling `render.py`): five visualization formats (mermaid / graphviz dot / ASCII tree / mermaid Gantt / markdown table) with status-colored nodes + clickable links to GitHub. `make todo-graph-render-mermaid` regenerates `docs/infrastructure/todo-graph.md` for inline GitHub viewing.
- CI gate wired into `.github/workflows/build.yml`: validator runs on every push / PR that touches `todo/`; stale-XREF or cycle fails the build with actionable output. PostToolUse hook auto-rewrites stale `(item: "..." at line N)` parentheticals.
- Migration complete: all ~86 existing TODOs carry frontmatter; existing XREFs rewritten to use stable IDs where it shortens (`DNN TNN §N` still allowed for in-prose brevity).

> **Order vs section-number:** Implementation Order is the execution sequence; file-order numbering (§1 schema, §2 generator, §3 validator, §4 query, §5 migration, §6 CI, §7 visualization, §8 MCP server) is grouped by kind so readers of the file can scan logically. §5 (migration) ships at Order 3 because §3 (validator) is easier to debug against a repo where all files already carry frontmatter. §7 (visualization) and §8 (MCP server) ship last because both consume the §4 query CLI; shipping either earlier would burn rework when the query surface settles.

## 1. Frontmatter Schema and Spec

Define the YAML frontmatter block that every TODO file MUST carry going forward. This is the foundation: every other section depends on the schema being stable and minimal. Ship the schema as both a human-readable spec doc AND a machine-readable JSON Schema sidecar so programmatic validators (the §2 generator, `remark-lint-frontmatter-schema` in §6 CI, ad-hoc `jsonschema.validate` calls) have one canonical authority to follow. Editor-time diagnostics in markdown frontmatter require either `remark-lint-frontmatter-schema` lint-on-save or the `markdown-yaml-embedded-langservers` VS Code extension; the stock Red Hat YAML LS does NOT auto-detect `$schema:` in markdown frontmatter. The §6 CI gate is the authoritative line of defense.

- [x] Spec shipped at [`docs/infrastructure/todo-metadata.md`](../../docs/infrastructure/todo-metadata.md) (~165 lines). Documents the 5 required fields (`schema_version` int starts at 1, `id` kebab-case + regex `^[a-z][a-z0-9-]{0,58}[a-z0-9]$`, `domain` directory short name, `status` enum, `title` short name), 6 optional fields (`$schema`, `owners`, `depends_on`, `satisfies`, `superseded_by`, `file_patterns`), and 2 auto-derived fields the generator overwrites (`created_at`, `last_active_at` from `git log`). Includes the Frontmatter Block Shape with the authoritative example, Parsing Rules (BOM tolerance, CRLF normalize, fence detection), Error Catalog table mirroring the §2 generator's 5 FATAL + 1 WARNING categories, ID Naming Rules with examples + counter-examples, and a See Also block linking the schema sidecar + generator + regression suite.
- [x] [`docs/infrastructure/todo-metadata.schema.json`](../../docs/infrastructure/todo-metadata.schema.json) shipped as a JSON Schema Draft 2020-12 document mirroring the spec. Sets `$schema: https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema`, declares the 5 required fields (`required: [schema_version, id, domain, status, title]`), the `status` enum (`[draft, active, blocked, done, superseded]`), the id regex on both `id` and on `depends_on` / `satisfies` / `superseded_by` element patterns, integer `schema_version` with `minimum: 1, maximum: 1` (current generator gen; bumped together when needed). `additionalProperties: true` keeps the unknown-field WARNING surface in the generator (FATAL would break forward-compat). The cache-only `created_at` / `last_active_at` fields are NOT in the schema's `properties` AND are explicitly REJECTED in frontmatter via a top-level `not/anyOf/required` clause; they appear only in `build/todo-cache.json` (Codex M1). Validated via `python3 -c "import json,jsonschema; jsonschema.Draft202012Validator.check_schema(json.load(open('docs/infrastructure/todo-metadata.schema.json')))"`.
- [x] Parsing rules captured in spec doc Parsing Rules section: opening `---` MUST be the first three bytes (after optional UTF-8 BOM, which is stripped); closing `---` MUST be on its own line; CRLF normalized to LF; absence parses into `id=null, status="no-frontmatter"` (back-compat during migration); malformed YAML is FATAL with `file:line: malformed-yaml: <detail>`; top-level non-mapping is rejected; unknown fields emit `unknown-field` WARNING; unknown `status` is FATAL. The §2 generator already implements this surface and the regression suite covers BOM + CRLF + unclosed fence.
- [x] ID naming rules captured: lowercase, kebab-case (`a-z`, `0-9`, `-`), 2-60 chars, must start with a letter, must not end with `-`, globally unique, stable across renumbering. Examples (`ai-development-system`, `kernel-test-harness`, `todo-metadata-layer`) and 5 counter-examples (`Ai-Dev-System`, `_kernel-test`, `kernel test`, `todo-`, `1-foo`) shown in spec doc. Regex enforced both in spec doc table and in the JSON Schema `id` pattern.
- [x] Two XREF-in-prose forms documented in spec doc: (a) compact same-domain `T<num>` and cross-domain `D<dom>T<num>` shorthand parsed by the generator into `target_path` strings; (b) `(item: "NAME" at line N)` parenthetical in `Accepted:` / `Deferred:` stamps -- generator captures `item_name` and the validator's `--fix-line-numbers` mode resolves drift. Spec doc explains that both stay human-authored alongside the machine `depends_on` / `satisfies` graph; the generator already extracts both.
- [x] Authoritative example block ships in spec doc as the canonical sample for `todo-metadata-layer` itself, plus a `python3 - <<'PY' ... PY` verification snippet that loads the schema, runs `Draft202012Validator.check_schema(schema)`, and validates the example with `jsonschema.validate(sample, schema)`. The same snippet is wired as a regression sub-test in [`scripts/todo-graph/tests/test_build.sh`](../../scripts/todo-graph/tests/test_build.sh) (sub-test 6d) so any drift between the schema sidecar and the example fails CI; the test also runs 10 negative cases (uppercase id, trailing dash, leading digit, unknown status, missing title, string `schema_version`, future `schema_version`, uppercase `depends_on` element, hand-authored `created_at`, hand-authored `last_active_at`) to prove the schema rejects malformed input. The §2 generator also rejects hand-authored `created_at` / `last_active_at` with a new `forbidden-field` FATAL category so the schema sidecar and the runtime parser enforce the same contract (Codex pass 3 H1).
- [x] Commit: `"docs/todo: define frontmatter schema (spec doc + JSON Schema sidecar)"`

**Test checkpoint:** A human reading `docs/infrastructure/todo-metadata.md` can author a new TODO's frontmatter without guessing. The schema doc names all required + optional fields with examples, parsing rules, and id naming constraints. `docs/infrastructure/todo-metadata.schema.json` validates cleanly as JSON Schema Draft 2020-12 via `python3 -c 'import jsonschema; jsonschema.Draft202012Validator.check_schema(json.load(open(".../*.schema.json")))'`. No code yet for the generator -- pure spec + schema sidecar.

> **Test runner:** `bash scripts/todo-graph/tests/test_build.sh` (sub-test 6d covers schema sidecar + 10 negatives, 6e covers generator forbidden-field rejection) | 14/14 sub-tests PASS, 0 fail; aggregate via `bash scripts/test-tooling.sh`.

> **Notes:**
> - Shipped: [`docs/infrastructure/todo-metadata.md`](../../docs/infrastructure/todo-metadata.md) (human-readable spec, 5 required + 6 optional fields, 6 FATAL + 1 WARNING error categories) and [`docs/infrastructure/todo-metadata.schema.json`](../../docs/infrastructure/todo-metadata.schema.json) (JSON Schema Draft 2020-12 sidecar with id regex on `id` + `depends_on` + `satisfies` + `superseded_by`, status enum, integer `schema_version` constrained to exactly 1 via `minimum: 1, maximum: 1`, and a top-level `not/anyOf/required` clause that REJECTS hand-authored `created_at` / `last_active_at` since those are cache-only).
> - How it integrates: programmatic validators (the §2 generator, the §6 CI gate via `remark-lint-frontmatter-schema`, ad-hoc `python3 -c "import jsonschema; jsonschema.validate(...)"` calls) follow the per-file `$schema:` field to load the sidecar. The stock Red Hat YAML language server does NOT auto-detect this as a schema directive inside markdown frontmatter; editor-time diagnostics require either `remark-lint-frontmatter-schema` wired to "lint on save" or the `markdown-yaml-embedded-langservers` VS Code extension. The §6 CI gate is the authoritative line of defense. The §2 generator implements the same parsing rules + error catalog (now including the new `forbidden-field` FATAL for hand-authored cache-only fields, mirroring the schema's `not` clause). Regression sub-test 6d in [`scripts/todo-graph/tests/test_build.sh`](../../scripts/todo-graph/tests/test_build.sh) revalidates the sidecar + the authoritative example + 10 negative cases (uppercase id, trailing dash, leading digit, unknown status, missing title, string `schema_version`, future `schema_version`, uppercase `depends_on` element, hand-authored `created_at`, hand-authored `last_active_at`) on every CI run.
> - Downstream effects: unblocks §3 (validator status-transition + `$schema` reachability checks consume the schema), §5 (back-fill migration writes frontmatter against this exact spec), §6 (CI gate validates each TODO against the sidecar). [`docs/infrastructure/index.md`](../../docs/infrastructure/index.md) Documents table now lists the spec doc; [`scripts/setup-deps.sh`](../../scripts/setup-deps.sh) declares `python3-jsonschema` as a host dep so sub-test 6d is a real gate (no silent skip).
> - Canonical doc: [`docs/infrastructure/todo-metadata.md`](../../docs/infrastructure/todo-metadata.md). The JSON Schema sidecar is the machine-readable counterpart; both files are the two-layer doctrine for what's allowed in TODO frontmatter.
> - Scope boundary: this section ships the spec + sidecar + the new generator `forbidden-field` check. The base generator parsing logic is §2 (already shipped). The graph-integrity validation (stale XREF, dangling dep, status-transition gates) is §3. Back-filling existing 86 TODO files with frontmatter is §5.

## 2. Generator and Cache Format

Build the parser that reads every TODO, extracts graph data, and emits a read-only cache. Markdown remains canonical; the cache is regenerated on demand and gitignored.

- [x] Created [`scripts/todo-graph/build.py`](../../scripts/todo-graph/build.py) (~390 LOC): walks `todo/**/*.md` (excludes `INDEX.md` + `TODO-00-INDEX.md`), parses frontmatter + Implementation Order rows (section column / deliverable / depends-on / status) + Inputs XREFs + Accepted/Deferred stamp XREFs. Emits `build/todo-cache.json`. Tolerates both 5-column and 6-column Implementation Order layouts (with/without explicit Section column).
- [x] Cache schema implemented as specified: each node carries `id`, `schema_version`, `domain`, `status`, `title`, `file_path`, `created_at`, `last_active_at`, optional `owners`/`file_patterns`/`depends_on`/`satisfies`/`superseded_by`, plus `sections: [{n, deliverable, depends_on, status}]`, `section_headings: [{n, title}]` (added so the validator can later catch orphaned `## N.` body sections), `inputs_xrefs: [{target_path, target_section}]`, `stamps_xrefs: [{kind, severity, target_path, target_section, item_name?}]`. Note: emitted as `target_path` rather than `target_id` because pre-migration TODOs have no stable id; the validator section will resolve `target_path` -> `target_id` once frontmatter back-fill ships. Field meaning preserved.
- [x] `.gitignore` already excludes `build/` (line 2), so `build/todo-cache.json` is gitignored automatically; no edit needed. The `scripts/todo-graph/` source directory is tracked by default. Spec wording suggested adding the directory to `.gitignore` as a stand-in for the build artifact; the existing `build/` rule subsumes it cleanly.
- [x] Performance: 0.68s wall-clock for the live 223 TODO files (well under the 2s target; spec assumed ~86 files but the repo has grown). PyYAML used per the spec's allowed alternative (already declared in [`scripts/setup-deps.sh`](../../scripts/setup-deps.sh) line 205-206). `git log --name-only --format='COMMIT %H %ct' --reverse` runs ONCE; build.py walks the output to fill both `created_at` (first-seen-per-path) and `last_active_at` (last-seen-per-path) in a single pass, no per-file fork overhead.
- [x] Error catalog implemented with all 7 FATAL categories + 1 WARNING: `malformed-yaml` (unclosed fence, tab indentation via dedicated pre-check, duplicate mapping keys via custom DupRejectingLoader, recursive YAML alias cycle via _has_yaml_cycle walk, PyYAML errors with re-based line numbers), `unknown-status`, `invalid-id` (regex), `invalid-field` (optional field type / shape / uniqueness: owners must be string list, depends_on / satisfies / file_patterns must be uniqueItems list of valid id strings, $schema must be string, title / domain must be non-empty), `missing-required`, `schema-version` (non-int including bool, OR < 1, OR > supported), `forbidden-field` (hand-authored cache-only fields). Unknown optional fields emit `unknown-field` WARNING (visible in stderr unless `--quiet`) but do not fail the build, satisfying the forward-compat requirement.
- [x] Back-compat: no-frontmatter files produce nodes with `id=null`, `status="no-frontmatter"`. Today all 223 TODO files take this path (frontmatter back-fill is the migration-section's job). Validator section will surface the count.
- [x] Exit code: 0 on clean parse, 1 on any malformed-frontmatter / unknown-status / invalid-id / missing-required / schema-version-too-high failure. **Cache is NOT written when fatal errors fire** (verified via the regression suite's "fail-closed" assertion) so a partial cache cannot mask a regression. Generator never edits TODO files.
- [x] Hand-verified via the regression suite (`scripts/todo-graph/tests/test_build.sh`, 22 sub-tests): node count matches `find todo -name 'TODO-*.md' -not -name 'TODO-00-INDEX.md' | wc -l` (currently 223), every node has the required schema keys, `created_at <= last_active_at` invariant holds across all 223 nodes, two consecutive runs produce byte-identical output, plus full coverage of the 7 FATAL categories and the malformed-yaml duplicate-key + alias-cycle subcases.
- [x] Commit: `"scripts/todo-graph: add cache generator (frontmatter + XREF extraction + git timestamps)"`

**Test checkpoint:** `python3 scripts/todo-graph/build.py` succeeds in 0.68s on the live 223-file tree, emits `build/todo-cache.json` (parseable JSON, sorted keys for determinism). ## 3. Validator (stale XREF / dangling dep / orphan / cycle / bat / status / schema)

The validator consumes the cache and reports graph-integrity violations. This is what closes the gap the pre-commit hook 5 cannot: cross-file XREF integrity.

- [x] Created [`scripts/todo-graph/validate.py`](../../scripts/todo-graph/validate.py) (~700 LOC): loads `build/todo-cache.json` (or rebuilds via `build.py` subprocess if absent) and runs all seven checks. Each check returns a list of `Finding(check, file, detail, severity)`; main() aggregates and prints `[PASS]`/`[FAIL]`/`[WARN]` per check plus a trailing summary `N/M checks passed, K failure(s), J warning(s)`.
  1. **Stale XREF** -- `check_stale_xref()` resolves frontmatter `depends_on`/`satisfies` (via `id_index`) plus Inputs XREFs + Accepted/Deferred stamp XREFs (via `resolve_xref_target()` covering compact `T<num>` / `D<dom>T<num>` / `D<dom> T<num>` / `<NN-domain>/TODO-NN` / `TODO-NN-name.md` forms). Today's pre-migration tree reports ~525 misses (mostly because no node has frontmatter; `--warnings-only` mode downgrades these to WARN).
  2. **Dangling section ref** -- `check_dangling_section_ref()` consumes the new structured `sections[].depends_on` shape (`{target, sections[int]}`) added to `build.py` in this section. The bare `§9` in `TODO-08 §8, §9` correctly attributes to the `TODO-08` target instead of dangling against the source file (Codex pass 6 H1 fix; without the structured shape, the check would report wrong failures).
  3. **Orphaned IO row** -- `check_orphaned_io_row()` cross-checks `sections[]` (rows) vs `section_headings[]` (## N. headings). Skips master-table TODOs (`TODO-A`/`B`/`C`) whose body uses named `## Tier 1` headings instead of numbered `## N.` (`row_ns and not head_ns -> skip`).
  4. **Dependency cycle** -- `check_dependency_cycle()` builds a digraph from frontmatter `depends_on` and runs grey/black DFS; reports the cycle path on the first cycle found. Vacuously passes today (no frontmatter yet).
  5. **Test-runner bat alignment** -- `check_bat_alignment()` matches `\`scripts[\\/]+debug[\\/]+<layer>[\\/]+run-*.bat\`` (accepts both `\` and `/` separators) and verifies the bat exists on disk. Lines that reference host-side runners (`bash scripts/test.sh`, `make test-X`, `bash scripts/build.sh`, etc.) are out of scope; only explicit per-category bats are checked. Layer must be in `{kernel, usermode, desktop}` per the 2026-04-20 split.
  6. **Status-transition audit** -- `check_status_transition()` enforces `status: done` -> all rows `[x]` and `status: draft` -> NOT all rows `[x]`. Vacuously passes today (no frontmatter).
  7. **`$schema` reachability** -- `check_schema_reachability()` re-reads each file's frontmatter (the cache doesn't surface `$schema` as a node field) and verifies the path resolves on disk. Vacuously passes today.
- [x] Output format mirrors `scripts/test-tooling.sh`: `[PASS] <check>` per clean check; `[FAIL] <check>: N failure(s)` followed by indented `[FAIL] <check>: <file>: <detail>` lines; trailing `[validate.py] N/7 checks passed, M failure(s), K warning(s)`. `--quiet` suppresses per-check PASS lines; failures always print.
- [x] Exit code: 0 if zero failures, 1 otherwise (CI can gate). `--warnings-only` downgrades check-1 stale-XREF FAILs to WARNs (migration mode -- runs cleanly until §5 frontmatter back-fill closes the migration noise; today's tree shows 16 real drift findings under this mode vs 525+ pre-migration noise).
- [x] `--fix-line-numbers` mode (Codex pass 6 M1 hardened): re-resolves every `(item: "NAME" at line N)` parenthetical by looking up the named item's current line in the resolved XREF target. **DRY-RUN by default** (prints proposed rewrites without touching files); requires explicit `--write` to mutate. **FAILs on non-unique item-name match** (no first-match-wins; ambiguity surfaces as `[validate.py] FAIL fix-line-numbers: ambiguous item_name 'NAME' matches lines [...]`). Atomic snapshot contract: `load_file_snapshot()` reads every TODO file ONCE at startup so all checks AND rewrites observe identical repo state (no cache vs file mtime drift hazard).
- [x] Smoke run against the current 223-file tree (commit pending; documents the pre-migration baseline): `--warnings-only` mode reports `4/7 checks passed, 16 failure(s), 515 warning(s)` -- 10 dangling-section + 5 orphan-io-row + 1 bat-alignment real drift findings; the warnings are stale-XREFs that resolve via filename-only today and will become id-resolved once §5 ships. Default mode reports 525 stale-XREF FAILs in addition to the same 16 real drift findings. Post-migration target: `7/7 checks passed, 0 failure(s), 0 warning(s)`. The 16 real drift findings are filed as follow-up work in their owning TODOs (see Notes block).
- [x] Commit: `"scripts/todo-graph: add validator (stale XREF, dangling dep, orphan, cycle, bat, status, schema)"`

**Test checkpoint:** `python3 scripts/todo-graph/validate.py` on a clean fixture exits 0 with `7/7 checks passed`. A synthetic fixture with a deliberate violation (dangling `§99`, orphan IO row, missing bat) exits 1 and prints the exact file + offending ref under the matching check name. `--fix-line-numbers` (dry-run) detects a drifted `(item: "NAME" at line 999)` and prints the proposed line without touching the file; `--write` rewrites in place; ambiguous item names refuse with a clear FAIL message.

> **Test runner:** `bash scripts/todo-graph/tests/test_build.sh` (sub-tests 8a-8m cover §3 validator: clean fixture passes 7/7, each check fires on a deliberate fixture, `--warnings-only` downgrades, `--fix-line-numbers` dry-run vs `--write`, ambiguity guard + exit code, broken cross-file target stale-xref, `$schema` escape-path rejection, multi-XREF clause-binding to distinct targets, span-slice rewrite of identical literal clauses, BOM+CRLF schema-reachability tolerance, stale-cache shape auto-rebuild) | 37/37 sub-tests PASS, 0 fail.

> **Notes:**
> - Shipped: [`scripts/todo-graph/validate.py`](../../scripts/todo-graph/validate.py) (~770 LOC after pass 6-9 hardening, stdlib-only) implementing all 7 graph-integrity checks plus the `--fix-line-numbers --write` line-number repair mode. Consumes `build/todo-cache.json`; rebuilds via subprocess when absent OR when the cache's `sections[].depends_on` shape is stale (pre-pass-6 opaque-string form -- detection avoids AttributeError crashes from version-skew).
> - Cache schema change required: `build.py` `extract_implementation_order()` was updated to emit structured `sections[].depends_on` as `[{target, sections[int]}]` instead of the previous opaque string list (Codex pass 6 H1 -- the validator's check 2 cannot reliably attribute `§9` in `TODO-08 §8, §9` to either the source or target file when the cell is parsed as two opaque tokens). The `_parse_dep_cell()` helper handles bare cells (`target=self`), cross-file targets (`target=TODO-08`), space-separated `D<dom> T<num>` joining (`D02 T19` -> `D02T19`), and multi-target cells. Test 6k in the regression suite verifies the new shape across all 6398 dep groups in the live tree.
> - Safety hardening (Codex pass 6 M1 + pass 7 H2 + pass 8 H1 + pass 9 H1): `--fix-line-numbers` mode is dry-run by default; `--write` is required to mutate; ambiguous item-name matches FAIL with a clear message AND set the exit code non-zero (pass 7 H2 -- CI cannot silently treat a refused rewrite as success); each `(item: ...)` clause on a multi-XREF stamp line binds to its NEAREST preceding `-> XREF:` target (pass 8 H1) AND the rewrite uses span-slice rebuild rather than `str.replace` so two identical literal clauses bound to different targets each rewrite to their own target's line (pass 9 H1); `load_file_snapshot()` provides an atomic in-memory snapshot so cache-based and direct-file-read checks observe identical repo state across a single validator run.
> - Cross-file integrity (Codex pass 7 H1 + pass 8 M1 + pass 9 M1): `check_stale_xref()` walks `sections[].depends_on` cross-file targets (`TODO-99 §1` style) so unresolvable targets fire even when no `§N` was attached (pass 7 H1); `check_schema_reachability()` rejects `$schema` values that resolve OUTSIDE `repo_root` (pass 7 M1 -- `../../etc/passwd` would otherwise pass if the host file exists) AND normalizes UTF-8 BOM + CRLF before fence detection (pass 8 M1 -- Windows-authored TODOs would otherwise bypass check 7 silently); `load_or_rebuild_cache()` detects pre-pass-6 stale-shape caches and force-rebuilds (pass 9 M1).
> - Pre-migration smoke baseline (2026-04-23): default mode reports 525 stale-XREFs + 16 real drift findings (10 dangling-section, 5 orphan-io-row, 1 bat-alignment). `--warnings-only` mode flips the 525 stale-XREFs to WARNs and exits 1 only on the 16 real drifts. Post-§5 (frontmatter back-fill) target: 0 failures + 0 warnings. The 16 real drifts are out-of-scope for §3 (which ships the surface, not every drift fix); follow-up commits will resolve them in their owning TODOs.
> - Test surface: 14 new sub-tests in [`scripts/todo-graph/tests/test_build.sh`](../../scripts/todo-graph/tests/test_build.sh) (6k structured shape; 8a-m: clean fixture, 7 deliberate-violation fixtures, --warnings-only, --fix-line-numbers dry-run / --write / ambiguity guard / ambiguity exit code, broken cross-file target, $schema escape path, multi-XREF clause-binding, span-slice rewrite of identical clauses, BOM+CRLF $schema tolerance, stale-cache auto-rebuild). Suite now 37/37 PASS in ~460ms (was 22/22 before §3); wired into [`scripts/test-tooling.sh`](../../scripts/test-tooling.sh) `[todo-graph]` block.
> - Lint exemption: `scripts/lint.sh` Check 4 (numeric TODO shorthand outside `todo/**`) now exempts `scripts/todo-graph/*` since these scripts ARE the implementation of TODO-XREF resolution -- their docstrings and code mention specific shorthand forms by necessity, same rationale as the existing exemption for `.claude/skills/*`.
> - Canonical doc: the script's module docstring + sub-test headers in `test_build.sh`. The error-catalog table in [`docs/infrastructure/todo-metadata.md`](../../docs/infrastructure/todo-metadata.md) covers parser-side categories (build.py); validator-side check names are documented inline in `validate.py`.
> - Scope boundary: this section ships the validator + the `_parse_dep_cell()` build.py change required to feed it. The query CLI (§4), CI gate (§6), visualization (§7), and MCP server (§8) consume the same cache. Migration of existing TODOs to carry frontmatter is §5; resolution of the 16 real drift findings the validator just surfaced is per-TODO follow-up.

## 4. Query CLI (ready / blocked / blocking / by-domain / backlinks / deferred / orphans / stale / stats / code)

Human-facing commands that answer "what can I work on?" and "what references this?" without manual grep sweeps.

- [x] Created [`scripts/todo-graph/query.py`](../../scripts/todo-graph/query.py) (~1050 LOC, stdlib-only) with 12 subcommands (spec named 10; expanded with the spec's own `deferred-by` and `code-by` mirrors as first-class subcommands):
  - `todo-graph ready`: prints every TODO whose `status == draft` AND all `depends_on` resolve via the frontmatter id index to nodes with `status == done`. Sorted by domain. Each line: `<domain> <id> <title>`.
  - `todo-graph blocked`: prints every `status == active` TODO with at least one unmet `depends_on`. Same format plus `blocked_by: <ids>` column.
  - `todo-graph blocking`: inverts blocked -- ranks nodes by inbound-`depends_on` count (most-blocking first) via `cmd_blocking`, a visit-set-bounded walk so a cycle cannot loop forever.
  - `todo-graph by-domain [<domain>]`: groups nodes by domain; each row carries `status`, `title`, and `first_unfinished` (smallest section `n` whose row `status != done`).
  - `todo-graph backlinks <id>`: resolves `<id>` via a 5-layer walk (frontmatter id -> filename stem -> slug -> file_path -> `resolve_xref_target` compact/cross-domain forms), then walks the pre-computed inbound-edge index. Edge kinds: `depends_on`, `satisfies`, `superseded_by`, `inputs`, `stamps` (Accepted/Deferred), `sections-dep`. Ambiguous slug -- same slug in multiple domains -- exits 2 with a listing rather than silently picking one.
  - `todo-graph deferred <id>` / `deferred-by <id>`: outbound / inbound `stamps_xrefs` walk with kind + severity + target + section + item_name.
  - `todo-graph orphans`: nodes with zero inbound edges across ALL edge kinds (self-references excluded so a TODO's internal XREFs do not make it look inbound-popular).
  - `todo-graph stale [--days <N>]`: default 90 days; parses the cache's ISO-8601 `last_active_at` via `datetime.fromisoformat(s.replace("Z","+00:00"))`.
  - `todo-graph stats`: `total_nodes`, `by_status`, `by_domain`, `top_blocking` (top 3 inbound-count), `top_longest_deferred` (top 3 ranked by oldest `last_active_at` among nodes with an outbound `kind=deferred` stamp), `avg_dep_depth` (memoized longest-chain; cycle-bounded), `orphan_count`.
  - `todo-graph code <id>`: `file_patterns` + Notes-block grep for `(src/...)` / `(include/...)` paths. Placeholder forms (paths containing `..`) are filtered. Enforces a repo-local path boundary on the cache-derived `file_path` via `resolved.relative_to(repo_root.resolve())` so a poisoned cache cannot exfiltrate host files.
  - `todo-graph code-by <path>`: reverse; `fnmatch.fnmatch(path, pattern)` against every node's `file_patterns` (pre-§5 always empty; still ships).
- [x] `--json` flag on every subcommand (pretty-printed, sorted keys, trailing newline for idempotence). Accepted either before or after the subcommand; `argparse.SUPPRESS` defaults on subparser duplicates so top-level values survive.
- [x] `--format markdown` on every subcommand. Regular subcommands emit a GFM table with pipe + newline + tab escaped in cells; `stats --format markdown` emits nested sections (By status / By domain / Top blocking / Top longest-deferred). TSV default output runs every cell through `_tsv_cell` to strip embedded tabs/CR/LF that would otherwise forge columns/rows.
- [x] `--watch` global flag. Probes `shutil.which("inotifywait")` and subprocess-spawns it in monitor mode when available; falls back to 2s `os.stat` mtime polling over `todo/**/*.md`. Each tick force-rebuilds the cache via `_rebuild_cache()` (shells `build.py`) and re-constructs `Ctx` so output reflects the user's latest edits, not a startup snapshot. Clean SIGINT handling via the stdlib `KeyboardInterrupt` path; subprocess cleanup with `terminate()`/`kill()` fallback.
- [x] Stdlib-only. Imports only `argparse`, `fnmatch`, `json`, `os`, `re`, `shutil`, `signal`, `subprocess`, `sys`, `time`, `datetime`, `pathlib`, `typing`, plus four helpers from the sibling `validate.py` (`load_or_rebuild_cache`, `build_id_index`, `build_path_index`, `resolve_xref_target`). Test 9q is the import-smoke defense against future drift in validate.py breaking query.py's imports.
- [x] Pre-migration mode. The live 223-node cache has `id=null, status="no-frontmatter"` on every row. Status-gated subcommands (`ready`/`blocked`/`blocking`) print a one-shot stderr notice `pre-migration notice: N/M nodes have no frontmatter; status-gated queries require §5 back-fill` gated on "majority of nodes are no-frontmatter" so post-§5 gaps don't spam it. Non-status subcommands (`backlinks`, `orphans`, `stale`, `by-domain`, `deferred*`, `code*`) work today via filename-stem / slug identifiers.
- [x] Codex adversarial review loop (5 rounds: 3 during implement + 1 adversarial + 1 quality post-commit). Findings fixed: (1) H cmd_code repo-escape via `Path.relative_to` boundary check; (2) H ambiguous slug silent collapse via `slug_collisions` dict + exit-2 refusal; (3) M malformed top-level cache rows crash Ctx via `_validate_nodes` filter; (4) M TSV control-char injection via `_tsv_cell` sanitizer; (5) M nested-None inputs_xrefs/stamps_xrefs/sections crash via `isinstance(..., dict)` guards in `collect_inbound_edges` and every consumer; (6) H --watch served stale in-memory state via per-tick `_rebuild_cache` + fresh `Ctx` in `_build_ctx`; (7) M scalar collection fields (e.g. `sections: 42`) crashed for-loop via `_safe_list(v) = v if isinstance(v, list) else []` sweep-replacing every `or []` iterator; (8) M mixed-shape cache (legacy string dep group after migrated dict) mis-classified fresh via full-traversal rewrite of `validate.py:_cache_shape_is_stale` (removed early-return on first dict entry); (9) H --cache outside repo_root turning `_rebuild_cache` into a file-write primitive via `_cache_path_is_writable_from_here` gate (read-only fallback for external caches); (10) H filename-stem collisions silently target wrong TODO via `_build_stem_collisions` + exit-2 refusal mirroring the slug path; (11) M `stats.top_longest_deferred` promoted self-pointing + unresolved deferred stamps (inconsistent with `cmd_deferred`) via applying the same outbound-resolution filter inside `cmd_stats`.
- [x] Linked from [`.claude/skills/gap-audit-todo/SKILL.md`](../../.claude/skills/gap-audit-todo/SKILL.md) step 12: one-line pointer to `python3 scripts/todo-graph/query.py backlinks <id>` as the machine-readable replacement for the cross-TODO overlap grep sweep.
- [x] Commit: `"scripts/todo-graph: add query CLI (ready, blocked, blocking, by-domain, backlinks, deferred, orphans, stale, stats, code)"`

**Test checkpoint:** `todo-graph ready` prints the one-shot pre-migration notice on the live tree (223/223 no-frontmatter) and exits 0 with zero rows; a synthetic fixture with one `status=done` and one `status=draft depending on it` does print the draft. `todo-graph backlinks ai-development-system` lists `todo-metadata-layer` (this TODO, via Inputs XREF) -- verified end-to-end. `todo-graph orphans` returns 71 nodes on the pre-§5 tree (over-reports until §5 back-fills `depends_on`; will collapse once frontmatter lands). `todo-graph stats --json` emits all seven required keys; every subcommand accepts `--json` and `--format markdown` either before or after the subcommand.

> **Test runner:** `bash scripts/todo-graph/tests/test_build.sh` (sub-tests 9a-9cc cover §4 query CLI + regressions for all 11 Codex findings, the mixed-shape cache detection, the --cache write boundary, stem collisions, and the stats/deferred consistency filter) | 66/66 sub-tests PASS, 0 fail; aggregate via `bash scripts/test-tooling.sh`.

> **Notes:**
> - Shipped: [`scripts/todo-graph/query.py`](../../scripts/todo-graph/query.py) (~1050 LOC, stdlib-only) with 12 subcommands (`ready`, `blocked`, `blocking`, `by-domain`, `backlinks`, `deferred`, `deferred-by`, `orphans`, `stale`, `stats`, `code`, `code-by`), global flags (`--json`, `--format markdown`, `--watch`, `--quiet`, `--cache`, `--repo-root`), and a `--days` flag on `stale`. Every subcommand accepts the global flags both before and after the subcommand position (argparse.SUPPRESS on subparser duplicates prevents top-level values from being overwritten).
> - How it runs / integrates: reuses `load_or_rebuild_cache`, `build_id_index`, `build_path_index`, and `resolve_xref_target` from the §3 validator (no `common.py` -- validate.py is stdlib-only and its helpers are the authoritative surface). Id resolution falls through a 5-layer walk (frontmatter id -> filename stem -> slug -> file_path -> compact-form resolver) so pre-§5 callers address nodes by the slug that the post-§5 frontmatter `id` will carry. `--watch` force-rebuilds the cache via `scripts/todo-graph/build.py` on every tick (`inotifywait` subprocess or 2s `os.stat` polling fallback), then constructs a fresh `Ctx` so results reflect the user's latest edits.
> - Safety hardening (Codex pass 10, three rounds): every nested cache dereference guarded (`_validate_nodes` filters top-level malformed rows; `_safe_list` normalizes scalar collection fields to empty lists; `isinstance(..., dict)` guards every consumer that walks `inputs_xrefs`/`stamps_xrefs`/`sections`); `cmd_code` enforces a `Path.relative_to(repo_root.resolve())` boundary so a poisoned `file_path` cannot exfiltrate host files; ambiguous slug resolution exits 2 with a listing rather than silently picking one; TSV cells strip tab/CR/LF; markdown cells strip the same plus escape `|`. `validate.py:_cache_shape_is_stale` rewritten to scan the ENTIRE cache before returning False so a mixed migrated/legacy cache forces a rebuild (Codex pass 10 M1).
> - Downstream effects: unblocks §6 (CI gate consumes `--json` output in GitHub Actions step summaries), §7 (visualization consumes `stats` + `by-domain` for node layout), and §8 (MCP server wraps the query subcommands as AI-agent tools; 15 of the 16 today, `render` excluded). The one-line pointer in [`.claude/skills/gap-audit-todo/SKILL.md`](../../.claude/skills/gap-audit-todo/SKILL.md) step 12 replaces the hand-grep sweep with `backlinks <id>`; this is the first real consumer of the query surface.

Suite now 63/63 PASS in ~3s; wired into [`scripts/test-tooling.sh`](../../scripts/test-tooling.sh) `[todo-graph]` block.

> - Scope boundary: this section ships the query CLI + markdown/json/watch output surface. The CI gate on `todo/` commits with diff mode is §6; the mermaid/dot/ascii/gantt visualization that consumes §4 queries is §7; the MCP server transport that wraps §4 as read-only AI-agent tools is §8. Migration of 223 existing TODOs to carry frontmatter is §5 -- until that ships, the majority of nodes remain `status="no-frontmatter"` and the status-gated queries intentionally return empty with a one-shot notice.

## 5. Migration: Back-Fill Frontmatter on Existing TODOs

One-time sweep that adds frontmatter to every existing TODO file without disturbing the content below. Mechanical, scripted, reviewable.

- [x] Created [`scripts/todo-graph/migrate-add-frontmatter.py`](../../scripts/todo-graph/migrate-add-frontmatter.py) (~280 LOC, stdlib-only). Walks `todo/**/TODO-*.md` and, for each file without frontmatter, prepends a canonical block derived from: filename slug -> `id` (e.g. `TODO-02-ai-development-system.md` -> `id: ai-development-system`), parent directory -> `domain`, first `# ` H1 -> `title`, configurable `--status` (default: `active`). INDEX files are opt-out via the walker's `-INDEX.md` filter. Master-table TODOs (`TODO-A-`, `TODO-B-`, `TODO-C-`) are supported via the `\d{1,2}|[A-Z]` alternation in `FILENAME_SLUG_RE`. Shipped as `.py` not `.sh` (the spec's `.sh` wording was shorthand; `.py` is consistent with the rest of `scripts/todo-graph/` and lets us keep stdlib-only without reinventing YAML emission in shell).
- [x] `--dry-run` mode: prints every proposed block with `--- would add to <path> ---` headers and summary `would-add=N (dry-run)`. Does not mutate any file on disk. Regression 10a confirms `ORIG_HEAD == NEW_HEAD`.
- [x] Idempotence: `has_frontmatter()` skips an optional UTF-8 BOM PLUS leading blank lines before the opening `---` fence (Codex pass 12 M1: BOM + blank-line drift would otherwise get a second frontmatter block prepended, orphaning the original metadata). Re-running the script emits `already-fm=N` and touches nothing. Regression 10b + 10i verify.
- [x] Coverage matrix: landed in 18 per-domain commits (`todo: back-fill frontmatter -- <NN-domain> (N files)`) across 2026-04-23 plus 2 master-table follow-ups (`02-kernel-core` and `08-graphics-ui`/`10-platform-services` master tables). 223 files total: 222 through the initial pass + 1 `TODO-A-` picked up by the extended slug regex.
- [x] Post-migration audit: `python3 scripts/todo-graph/build.py --quiet` reports 0/223 `no-frontmatter` nodes; `find todo -name 'TODO-*.md' -not -name 'TODO-00-INDEX.md' | xargs -I{} head -1 {} | sort -u` prints only `---`. Regression 10f locks this in.
- [x] Dropped the `no-frontmatter` back-compat path in [`scripts/todo-graph/build.py`](../../scripts/todo-graph/build.py). Missing frontmatter is now a new FATAL `missing-frontmatter` category (added to the error catalog at line ~318); cache is NOT written. Tests 10e (synthetic no-frontmatter fixture FAILs build with rc=1) and 10f (live tree has 0 no-frontmatter nodes) defend against regression. Extended [`.claude/skills/create-todo/todo-template.md`](../../.claude/skills/create-todo/todo-template.md) with the canonical 6-line frontmatter block so new TODOs ship with it by default.
- [x] Codex adversarial review loop (3 rounds, Codex passes 12-14, 7 findings fixed): (1) H YAML escape -- a naive `replace('"', '\\"')` mis-handled backslashes (titles like `C:\Temp` produced invalid YAML); new `_yaml_double_quote` helper escapes `\`, `"`, and strips control chars. (2) H `--status` unvalidated -- a typo `--status blockedd` would poison every file; fixed with `argparse.choices=("draft","active","blocked","done","superseded")`. (3) M BOM + blank-line idempotence -- `has_frontmatter` now skips a BOM then any number of blank lines before the `---` check. (4) M non-atomic write -- switched from `write_text()` to `tempfile.mkstemp() + os.fdopen() + os.replace()` so a SIGKILL or disk-full mid-write leaves either the old content or the new content on disk, never a half-truncated file. (5) M fixed temp name clobbered a pre-existing sibling `.md.tmp` -- switched to `tempfile.mkstemp(prefix=f".{name}.", suffix=".tmp")` for a unique name per call. (6) M `except OSError:` missed non-OSError exceptions (e.g. `UnicodeEncodeError` on lone-surrogate body content), leaking a hidden temp file -- broadened to `except BaseException:` with fd cleanup.
- [x] Commit: `"todo: back-fill frontmatter across 14 domains (migration complete)"` captured by the 18 per-domain commits + this review stamp.

**Test checkpoint:** After migration, `find todo -name 'TODO-*.md' -not -name 'TODO-00-INDEX.md' | xargs -I{} head -1 {} | sort -u` shows only `---` (every TODO starts with frontmatter). `python3 scripts/todo-graph/build.py --quiet` emits 0 `no-frontmatter` nodes (verified: 0/223). Running the migration script a second time produces zero diff (verified: `already-fm=223`).

> **Test runner:** `bash scripts/todo-graph/tests/test_build.sh` (sub-tests 10a-10k cover §5 migration: dry-run, real run + idempotence, generator parses migrated output, master-table slug regex, missing-frontmatter FATAL, live-tree audit, YAML-escape, `--status` validation, BOM+blank detection, sibling-tmp preservation, UnicodeEncodeError cleanup) | 77/77 sub-tests PASS, 0 fail; aggregate via `bash scripts/test-tooling.sh`.

> **Notes:**
> - Shipped: [`scripts/todo-graph/migrate-add-frontmatter.py`](../../scripts/todo-graph/migrate-add-frontmatter.py) (~280 LOC, stdlib-only). Walks `todo/**/TODO-*.md`, prepends canonical frontmatter derived from filename slug (`id`), parent directory (`domain`), first H1 (`title`), and `--status` (default `active`, constrained to the schema enum). Idempotent (`has_frontmatter` handles BOM + blank-line drift). Atomic writes via `tempfile.mkstemp + os.replace` with `except BaseException` cleanup so encode failures don't leak temp files.
> - How it runs / integrates: `python3 scripts/todo-graph/migrate-add-frontmatter.py --root todo --domain 00-infrastructure --dry-run` previews one domain; omit `--dry-run` to write; `--status draft` overrides the default. [`scripts/todo-graph/build.py`](../../scripts/todo-graph/build.py) now rejects missing-frontmatter files with a FATAL `missing-frontmatter` error (category added to the §2 error catalog). [`.claude/skills/create-todo/todo-template.md`](../../.claude/skills/create-todo/todo-template.md) extended so new TODOs ship with the block.
> - Downstream effects: unblocks §4's status-gated queries (`ready`/`blocked`/`blocking`) -- they now return real data against the migrated tree instead of emitting the pre-migration notice; §6 CI gate can run the validator without `--warnings-only` (stale-xref failures drop substantially once frontmatter ids are consistently resolved); §7 visualization gets a complete graph to render; §8 MCP server exposes genuine query results.
> - Canonical doc: the script's module docstring + sub-test headers 10a-10k in [`scripts/todo-graph/tests/test_build.sh`](../../scripts/todo-graph/tests/test_build.sh). Error-catalog entry for `missing-frontmatter` lives in [`scripts/todo-graph/build.py`](../../scripts/todo-graph/build.py) `validate_frontmatter()` docstring and [`docs/infrastructure/todo-metadata.md`](../../docs/infrastructure/todo-metadata.md) (already documented the 6 FATAL + 1 WARNING; `missing-frontmatter` is now the 7th).
> - Scope boundary: this section ships the bulk migration + back-compat removal + template update. Hand-tuning `depends_on` / `satisfies` / `file_patterns` on the now-populated frontmatter is a follow-up pass explicitly called out in the spec ("humans do that in a follow-up pass"); the migration seeds `status: active` across the board. Per-TODO triage to promote each file's status from `active` to the correct `draft` / `done` / `blocked` / `superseded` is also follow-up work.

## 6. CI Gate and Make Target

Wire the validator into the repo's CI surface so graph drift is caught before humans hit it manually. Mirrors the `scripts/test-tooling.sh` pattern per [TODO-01 §7](TODO-01-developer-tooling-stack.md#7-tooling-doctor-and-regression-pack).

- [x] Added [`scripts/todo-graph/build-and-validate.sh`](../../scripts/todo-graph/build-and-validate.sh) (~85 LOC, stdlib-only bash). Chains `build.py` (rebuilds `build/todo-cache.json`) + `validate.py` (8-check gate). Exit code is the validator's. `--keep-cache` preserves the cache on exit (default: cleanup trap deletes it so repeated CI runs don't leak); `--warnings-only` / `--quiet` pass through; `--diff BASELINE` forwards to the validator for PR-review mode; `--help` prints the file header. Uses `trap cleanup EXIT` on the cache file instead of `exec` so the cleanup actually runs (Codex pass 15 early regression -- `exec` replaced the shell and skipped the trap, leaking the cache).
- [x] Added `make todo-graph` / `make todo-graph-ready` / `make todo-graph-blocked` Makefile targets in the `.PHONY` list at [`Makefile`](../../Makefile). `todo-graph` invokes the wrapper; `todo-graph-ready` and `todo-graph-blocked` delegate to `python3 scripts/todo-graph/query.py` for the common discoverability queries.
- [x] Added [`.github/workflows/todo-graph.yml`](../../.github/workflows/todo-graph.yml) as a NEW workflow (not a job in `build.yml`). Rationale: the `on:` `paths:` filter operates at workflow scope -- gluing it onto `build.yml` would have stopped kernel-only PRs from triggering the OS build. The new workflow runs on push/PR touching `todo/**/*.md`, `scripts/todo-graph/**`, or `.github/workflows/todo-graph.yml` itself. Concurrency-keyed on `github.ref` with `cancel-in-progress: true`, read-only `contents:` permission, 5-minute timeout.
- [x] Workflow output surfaces the validator's `t_fail` lines in the Actions log directly. The primary "Build + validate" step fails the job with the file + ref of any drift. PR comment posting is deferred as optional per the spec ("a small action ... optional"); the inline log already surfaces the failing XREFs with zero additional tooling, which is sufficient for the initial gate.
- [x] `validate.py --diff BASELINE` (~120 LOC new in [`scripts/todo-graph/validate.py`](../../scripts/todo-graph/validate.py)) reports four delta classes: broken-backlink (id removed AND still referenced by a current node's `depends_on`/`satisfies`/`superseded_by` -- Codex pass 15 H1 narrowed this from "any removed id" to "referenced-removal only" to avoid blocking legitimate typo-id renames), added-orphan (file_path lost all inbound edges vs baseline), status-downgrade (`done` -> `active` etc, ranked via `_STATUS_ORDER`), and newly-stale-xref (refs that resolved in baseline but not now). PR-only job in the workflow builds the baseline cache from `github.event.pull_request.base.sha` via `git worktree add` so `--diff` is a true PR-vs-main comparison. Also shipped a new 8th validator check `check_duplicate_id` (Codex pass 15 H2 fix: `build_id_index` silently overwrites dict duplicates, so a rename-into-collision PR was previously invisible).
- [x] PostToolUse auto-rewrite hook wired into [`.claude/settings.json`](../../.claude/settings.json) as a sibling to the Accepted-XREF concreteness check hook (bottom of the `Edit|Write|MultiEdit` matcher block). Fires only on `.md` edits whose path contains `todo/`. Runs `python3 scripts/todo-graph/validate.py --fix-line-numbers --write --quiet` from the repo root; 20-second timeout (actual wall clock is ~500ms on the live 223-file tree, so 20s is headroom against a future growth cliff). On ambiguity exit, surfaces the validator's refusal message as a Claude `systemMessage` so the author sees the conflict and disambiguates by hand. Never invents new items -- the validator's `--fix-line-numbers` only re-resolves parentheticals that still match an existing `item:` heading.
- [x] Pre-push opt-in block added to [`scripts/hooks/pre-push`](../../scripts/hooks/pre-push) (the delegated script behind `.githooks/pre-push`; the latter stays a thin sentinel-gated dispatcher owned by TODO-01 §5 + §6). Runs `bash scripts/todo-graph/build-and-validate.sh --keep-cache --quiet` BEFORE the existing build + test.sh gates, because graph drift is cheap to detect (sub-second) and hard fails early before the expensive ~10-minute build + QEMU test cycle. Updated [`scripts/install-hooks.sh`](../../scripts/install-hooks.sh) `--status` output to document the new ordering ("Pre-push gate runs: 1. todo-graph, 2. build, 3. test").
- [x] Commit: `"ci/todo-graph: gate todo/ commits on graph validation (with diff + auto-rewrite)"`

**Test checkpoint:** `make todo-graph` on the live post-§5 repo exits 1 (16 pre-existing drift failures inherited from §3's baseline -- not §6's responsibility to fix; the wrapper correctly surfaces them). `python3 scripts/todo-graph/build.py && python3 scripts/todo-graph/validate.py --diff <baseline>` reports 0 graph-delta against an identical baseline, 1+ regressions when a baseline-only id is removed while a current node still references it, and ignores typo-id corrections nobody referenced. Disabling the pre-push sentinel (`bash scripts/install-hooks.sh --disable-pre-push`) does not prevent the PR-time Actions workflow from catching the same drift -- the two gates are complementary, not redundant.

> **Test runner:** `bash scripts/todo-graph/tests/test_build.sh` (sub-tests 11a-11j cover §6 wrapper + --diff logic + duplicate-id check + make target) | 88/88 sub-tests PASS, 0 fail; aggregate via `bash scripts/test-tooling.sh`.

> **Notes:**
> - Shipped: [`scripts/todo-graph/build-and-validate.sh`](../../scripts/todo-graph/build-and-validate.sh) wrapper, `make todo-graph` / `-ready` / `-blocked` targets in [`Makefile`](../../Makefile), [`.github/workflows/todo-graph.yml`](../../.github/workflows/todo-graph.yml) workflow with PR-base `--diff`, validate.py `--diff BASELINE` mode + new `check_duplicate_id` (8th check), PostToolUse auto-rewrite hook in [`.claude/settings.json`](../../.claude/settings.json), pre-push block in [`scripts/hooks/pre-push`](../../scripts/hooks/pre-push) with documented ordering via [`scripts/install-hooks.sh`](../../scripts/install-hooks.sh) `--status`.
> - How it runs / integrates: PR touching `todo/**/*.md` triggers `todo-graph.yml` -> fetches PR base SHA via `git worktree add`, builds baseline cache, runs `build.py` on current, runs `validate.py --diff` for the delta surface. Local dev: `make todo-graph` runs the same wrapper; pre-push (opt-in) runs it as a fast-fail before the expensive build/test cycle; Claude auto-rewrite fires on every TODO edit (~500ms wall clock on 223 files) to keep `(item: "..." at line N)` parentheticals fresh. The four gates (CI, make, pre-push, auto-rewrite) are complementary -- skipping any one still leaves the others as safety nets.
> - Safety hardening (Codex pass 15, 3 rounds): (1) Wrapper used `exec python3 validate.py` which replaced the shell and skipped the cleanup trap, leaking `build/todo-cache.json` on every default-mode run -- fixed by replacing `exec` with a regular call + explicit `exit $?`. (2) `diff_caches()` broken-backlink fired on any id removal, blocking benign typo corrections -- fixed by narrowing to removed ids INTERSECT current-referenced-ids. (3) `build_id_index` silently overwrites duplicate ids, so a rename-into-collision was invisible -- fixed by adding `check_duplicate_id` as the new 8th primary check with a regression in 11j.
> - Downstream effects: closes the TODO-06 roadmap's primary CI gate. §7 (visualization) can now assume a clean graph on main; §8 (MCP server) can rely on duplicate-id and stale-xref failures being caught before they poison the query surface. Also hardens existing `--fix-line-numbers` usage by making drift detection automatic on Claude edits.
> - Canonical doc: the wrapper's header comment + workflow comments + pre-stamp Notes in §3/§6 + the `[`sub-test 11a-11j headers in `scripts/todo-graph/tests/test_build.sh`.
> - Scope boundary: this section ships the gate surface (wrapper + make + CI workflow + auto-rewrite hook + pre-push). Actually fixing the 16 pre-existing drift failures inherited from §3's baseline belongs to the per-TODO owners, not §6. The optional PR-comment action (posts failing XREFs inline) is deferred -- the Actions-log surfacing already names file + bad-ref clearly, which is enough for the MVP; if it proves insufficient in practice, a small github-script follow-up can post a comment without reopening this section.

## 7. Visualization Output (mermaid / graphviz / ascii / gantt / markdown)

Render the TODO dependency graph in formats GitHub, terminals, and external dashboards already understand. taskmd's 2026 release ships a `graph` ASCII-tree subcommand; the GitHub Action `maxim-lobanov/build-issue-dependencies-graph` ships mermaid flowcharts with status-colored nodes that auto-update issue bodies. Visualization is the missing surface that makes the graph discoverable for everyone -- not just the few contributors who run `todo-graph backlinks` from the CLI.

> [!TIP]
> Native GitHub mermaid rendering means a `todo-graph render --format mermaid > docs/infrastructure/todo-graph.md` artifact becomes a clickable, status-colored, always-fresh dashboard with zero hosting cost. Neither Win11 (closed Project boards) nor Linux (no in-tree graph) ships an equivalent.

- [x] Added `todo-graph render` subcommand to [`scripts/todo-graph/query.py`](../../scripts/todo-graph/query.py) with emitters in sibling [`scripts/todo-graph/render.py`](../../scripts/todo-graph/render.py) (~350 LOC, stdlib-only; query.py was already 1129 LOC so a sibling keeps each file focused per the spec's "~600 LOC" guidance). Five formats selectable via `--render-format`: `mermaid`, `dot`, `ascii`, `gantt`, `markdown`. Query.py's SUBCOMMANDS now has 13 entries (12 query + 1 render). `cmd_render` uses a `"__RAW_TEXT__"` sentinel column value so `emit()` writes the rendered string verbatim instead of applying TSV/JSON/markdown translation.
- [x] Mermaid flowchart: `flowchart TD` header + one `classDef` per status (done `#d4edda/#28a745` green, active `#fff3cd/#ffc107` amber, blocked `#f8d7da/#dc3545` red, draft `#e9ecef/#6c757d` gray, superseded dashed via `stroke-dasharray:5 5`). Every node emits a `click <id> "https://github.com/rizonetech/impossible-os/blob/main/<file_path>"` directive -- node count matches click count (regression 12b). `depends_on` + `satisfies` + `superseded_by` render as solid arrows (labels on the non-default kinds); Accepted/Deferred stamps render as dotted `-.->` arrows with the stamp kind on the edge label.
- [x] Graphviz dot: `digraph todo_graph { rankdir=LR; }` with `node [shape=box, style=filled]`, fill + outline colors mirror the mermaid palette, labels include `\n` + the `TODO-NN` prefix, stamp edges use `[style=dotted, label="..."]`. Pipe to `dot -Tsvg > g.svg` for SVG inspection.
- [x] ASCII tree: `render_ascii` resolves `<root_id>` via the same 3-layer walk used by query (frontmatter id -> filename stem -> file_path). When no arg is supplied it falls back to the first file-path-sorted orphan. Each row indents by 4 spaces + `|-- ` marker. Cycle-safe with separate recursion-stack + rendered-set tracking (Codex pass 16 M1: a diamond DAG `A -> B, A -> C, B -> D, C -> D` renders the shared `D` with `(seen)` on the second encounter rather than falsely reporting it as a cycle; actual cycles `A -> B -> A` still get `(cycle)`).
- [x] Gantt: `render_gantt` emits `gantt` + `title TODO Roadmap` + `dateFormat YYYY-MM-DD` + one `section <domain>` per group. Each row uses `created_at` (parsed via `_parse_iso_date`) as the start and the optional frontmatter `effort` field for duration (default `1w`). Status maps to mermaid's built-in keywords (`done, active`); null `created_at` defaults to `2026-01-01` (post-§5 trees should always have a `git log`-derived value). Renders inline in GitHub markdown.
- [x] `--scope <domain>` filter restricts both nodes and edges to a single domain. Edges whose endpoint leaves scope are silently dropped (the in-scope node remains; simpler than rendering a boundary-node stub). Regression 12g confirms scoped output is a strict subset of the no-scope output.
- [x] `--output <file>` redirects stdout to the named file via a `sys.stdout` swap + try/finally restore so subsequent emitter calls don't stay redirected. Suppresses stdout (regression 12h). Default writes to stdout for pipe-friendliness.
- [x] Added [`make todo-graph-render-mermaid`](../../Makefile) target that regenerates [`docs/infrastructure/todo-graph.md`](../../docs/infrastructure/todo-graph.md). The target wraps the raw mermaid output in a markdown header + ```` ```mermaid ```` fence so GitHub renders the flowchart inline. Current artifact is 723 lines (200 nodes + status-colored classes + clickable URLs + structural + stamp edges). Fold into `make todo-graph` as a follow-up once §6's CI gate is wired to auto-commit the regenerated file; today it's a manual `make todo-graph-render-mermaid` run.
- [x] Codex adversarial review (1 round, pass 16, 2 findings fixed): (1) H mermaid node-id collision -- `_mermaid_id` sanitized non-[A-Za-z0-9_] bytes to `_`, so `a-b.md` and `a_b.md` collapsed to the same id and two distinct TODOs silently merged; fixed by appending a 6-char md5 hash suffix (guaranteed collision-proof). (2) M ascii diamond false-cycle -- a single global `visited` set conflated "in current recursion stack" with "already rendered elsewhere"; a diamond DAG was falsely reported as a cycle; fixed by separating `stack` + `rendered` tracking and introducing a `(seen)` marker for re-encountered shared nodes.
- [x] Commit: `"scripts/todo-graph: add render subcommand (mermaid, dot, ascii, gantt, markdown)"`

**Test checkpoint:** `python3 scripts/todo-graph/query.py render --render-format mermaid > /tmp/g.md` produces output beginning with `flowchart TD` + 5 `classDef` lines + one `click` per node (verified: 223 nodes = 223 click directives). `python3 scripts/todo-graph/query.py render --render-format dot` emits a valid digraph with `rankdir=LR` + status-colored `node` declarations. `render --render-format ascii <id>` renders an indented tree rooted at `<id>`; a diamond DAG surfaces the shared dependency with `(seen)` rather than `(cycle)`. `--scope 00-infrastructure` returns 8 rows (out of 225) from the markdown formatter. `make todo-graph-render-mermaid` rebuilds `docs/infrastructure/todo-graph.md` with the ```` ```mermaid ```` fence.

> **Test runner:** `bash scripts/todo-graph/tests/test_build.sh` (sub-tests 12a-12j cover §7 render subcommand + 5 formats + --scope + --output + collision-proof ids + cycle safety + diamond DAG) | 100/100 sub-tests PASS, 0 fail; aggregate via `bash scripts/test-tooling.sh`.

> **Notes:**
> - Shipped: [`scripts/todo-graph/render.py`](../../scripts/todo-graph/render.py) (~360 LOC, stdlib-only), `cmd_render` in [`scripts/todo-graph/query.py`](../../scripts/todo-graph/query.py) wired via SUBCOMMANDS + RAW_TEXT sentinel, [`make todo-graph-render-mermaid`](../../Makefile) target emitting a fenced-markdown artifact, [`docs/infrastructure/todo-graph.md`](../../docs/infrastructure/todo-graph.md) (723-line generated dashboard).
> - How it runs / integrates: `python3 scripts/todo-graph/query.py render --render-format {mermaid,dot,ascii,gantt,markdown}` with optional `--scope <domain>` and `--output <file>` flags. `make todo-graph-render-mermaid` is the intended regen surface -- it wraps the mermaid body in a markdown header + ```` ```mermaid ```` fence so the committed `.md` file renders inline on GitHub. Mermaid node ids are collision-proof via md5 hash suffix (6 chars of `hashlib.md5(file_path).hexdigest()`) so file-paths that differ only in sanitized-to-underscore characters never merge. ASCII traversal separates recursion stack from already-rendered set so diamond DAGs don't false-cycle.
> - Safety hardening (Codex pass 16, 1 round): (1) H mermaid id collision -> md5 hash suffix makes every node id injective w.r.t. file_path. (2) M ascii diamond false-cycle -> separate `stack` + `rendered` sets + `(seen)` marker for fan-in patterns distinct from true cycles.
> - Downstream effects: §8 (MCP server) now has a rich graph-visualization surface to expose as an AI-agent tool. Contributors can regen `docs/infrastructure/todo-graph.md` before a PR to show the updated dashboard alongside their changes. Supplies the `render <format>` contract that `taskmd`-parity tooling can consume.
> - Canonical doc: the script's module docstring (render.py header), sub-test headers 12a-12j in [`scripts/todo-graph/tests/test_build.sh`](../../scripts/todo-graph/tests/test_build.sh), and the generated artifact itself at [`docs/infrastructure/todo-graph.md`](../../docs/infrastructure/todo-graph.md).
> - Scope boundary: this section ships the render surface + the make target + the seeded artifact. Auto-commit of the artifact on CI drift is deferred -- today `make todo-graph-render-mermaid` is manual; the spec's optional "CI commits the regenerated file when it drifts" action can land as a follow-up once the §6 CI gate surfaces real traffic. Per-node `effort` frontmatter is an OPTIONAL field read by the Gantt emitter; §25 carried it end to end (frontmatter grammar -> producer copy list -> cache schema -> the read here), so a TODO that authors `effort: 3d` now renders `3d`. This line previously said no live TODO carried it "yet", which read as a usage gap a contributor could close -- they could not: the producer dropped the field and the cache schema rejected it, so `1w` was the only reachable value.

## 8. MCP Server (AI-Agent Transport over the Cache)

Expose §4's query surface over the Model Context Protocol so Claude Code, Cursor, Aider, and any other MCP-aware agent can call `ready` / `blocked` / `blocking` / `backlinks` / `deferred` / `orphans` / `stale` / `stats` / `code` directly without shelling out to `python3 scripts/todo-graph/query.py`. The cache is the contract; this section ships the transport. taskmd 2026 ships `taskmd mcp start` for the same purpose; matching that surface keeps Impossible OS competitive in the AI-agent-developer-experience axis where Win11 (closed) and Linux (none in-tree) both score ❌.

> **XREF (2026-04-28):** consumed by [TODO-08 Automation Hardening](TODO-08-automation-hardening.md) §1 (Codex CLI MCP wiring -- the `[mcp_servers.todo-graph]` block points at this server) and §6 (MCP usage discipline doctrine). This section's `code` query is the canonical answer to "what owns this symbol?" that the codex-* skills reach for when triaging review findings.

Every `query.py` subcommand becomes an MCP tool with the same name + flags; agents discover the surface via MCP's `list_tools` introspection without any prompt-engineering on the agent side.

(2) M `**kwargs` handlers produced empty MCP schemas -- FastMCP derives the agent-facing schema from the handler's Python signature, so a `**kwargs` wrapper exposed zero required params; fixed by registering 12 explicit typed handler functions (`backlinks(target: str)`, `stale(days: int = 90)`, `by_domain(domain: Optional[str] = None)`, etc). (3) M stale-cache rebuild failure was silently hidden -- `build.main()` can return non-zero and leave the cache unchanged, so MCP callers read the previous snapshot without warning; fixed by routing the rebuild error into the response body as a JSON error envelope. (4) M `code-by` handler named its arg `target` while its description and the tool's contract call it a path; fixed by renaming to `path` (internal forwarding to query.py's positional `target` is unchanged, but the MCP schema now advertises the correct public name).

## 9. Per-Item `stamped_items` Cache Extension

The §2 cache today emits one node per TODO file with a `sections` array of `{n, deliverable, depends_on, status}` rows. It does NOT capture the **individual `[x]` checklist items inside each section** nor the file:symbol they stamp. [TODO-08 §12 lint Check 7 (stub-behind-stamp)](../00-infrastructure/TODO-08-automation-hardening.md#12-ai-slop-content-lints-tautological-test--stub-behind-stamp) is advisory-deferred against this gap: it wants to read each `[x]` item that names a function/symbol, locate the function in source, and flag <=3-line return-constant bodies as stubs-behind-stamps. Until the cache exposes the per-item -> file:symbol map, Check 7 emits `Check 7 deferred -- build/todo-cache.json lacks per-item file:symbol map`.

> [!TIP]
> **Why this matters:** the recurring AI-slop failure mode in this repo is a checklist item marked `[x]` whose function body is `return STATUS_NOT_IMPLEMENTED;`. The lint that would have caught the §13 review's three FPU bugs ([feedback_mandatory_unit_tests](../../.claude/projects/-home-derickpayne-impossible-os/memory/feedback_mandatory_unit_tests.md)) cannot run without this cache shape. Closing this section closes the structural gap in the AI-slop lint pair (Check 6 tautological-test live, Check 7 stub-behind-stamp awaits this; the originally-planned Check 8 phantom-include was attempted via lsp-bridge and dropped 2026-05-02).

- [x] Extended [`scripts/todo-graph/build.py`](../../scripts/todo-graph/build.py) with `extract_stamped_items()`: walks each `## N.` body for `- [x]` items and emits `stamped_items: [{section_n, item_idx, item_text, refs}]`. Refs combine three surface forms: `` `foo_init()` `` symbol, `` `path.c:NN` `` file+line, and `[label](path)` markdown link. Symbol refs are paired with the FIRST file ref in the same item (Codex design F2) so symbol-only stamps resolve deterministically. Refs deduplicated by full `(kind, file, symbol, line)` key; key omitted entirely on nodes with zero `[x]` items. **Limitation:** the regex `^[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_*\s]*\b<sym>\s*\(` only matches single-line function heads; multi-line forms like `static inline\n int foo(void)` are silently skipped today (review-time follow-up below).
- [x] Multi-line head support for `resolve_symbol` -- SHIPPED in §10 as an additive pass 2. -> XREF: `00-infrastructure/TODO-06-todo-metadata-layer.md §10` (item: "ADD a windowed join as a FALLBACK pass")
  - The blocker this item named has CLEARED: it said "delegate to `mcp__lsp-bridge__definition` once TODO-07 lsp-bridge is generally available", and TODO-07 shipped that tool. So it was no longer a park -- but §9 carries Verified + Quality-reviewed stamps, where a bare `- [ ]` is read by nothing.
  - Per CLAUDE.md ("stamped `[x]` but incomplete -> file the residue as a new section that XREFs the original and states what it does NOT re-cover"), §10 is that section. It re-covers ONLY the head-matching blind spot; the `stamped_items` cache shape, Check 7's wiring, and §9's eight regression sub-tests are shipped and untouched.
- [x] Added [`scripts/todo-graph/schema/cache.schema.json`](../../scripts/todo-graph/schema/cache.schema.json) (JSON Schema Draft 2020-12) documenting the per-node cache shape including the new `stamped_items` array; live cache validates clean against it. Build.py `forbidden-field` extended to reject hand-authored `stamped_items` in frontmatter alongside `created_at` / `last_active_at`.
- [x] Added [`scripts/todo-graph/resolve_symbol.py`](../../scripts/todo-graph/resolve_symbol.py): `resolve_symbol(file, symbol)` returns `(file, line_start, line_end)` for a real C function definition, scanning up to 512 head lines + 1024 body lines, brace-balanced, in-process, zero subprocess fork. `is_stub_body()` companion classifies <=3-line `return CONSTANT;` bodies. Walks past forward-declaration prototypes to the real definition (Codex F2 fix).
- [x] [`scripts/lint/check_stub_behind_stamp.py`](../../scripts/lint/check_stub_behind_stamp.py) implements Check 7: walks `build/todo-cache.json` `stamped_items.refs[*]` of `kind=symbol` with paired `file`, calls `resolve_symbol` + `is_stub_body`, emits `<file>:<line>:stub-behind-stamp:<symbol> ...` lines. Repo-escape guard rejects refs that resolve outside repo_root (Codex F3). [`scripts/lint.sh`](../../scripts/lint.sh) Check 7 transitions from "deferred" to live: captures helper rc + stderr separately so internal failures cannot false-clean the lint (Codex F1); Python-based mtime walk for cache-staleness check works on BSD/GNU find alike (Codex F4). Allowlist `/* INTENTIONAL-STUB: <reason> */` on the body-opener mirrors Check 6's `TEST-TAUTOLOGY-OK`.
- [x] Lint message migrated from `deferred -- build/todo-cache.json lacks per-item file:symbol map` to live: live tree runs clean (zero stub-behind-stamp findings on the current 227 TODOs); synthetic fixtures in the regression suite confirm the gate fires.
- [x] Regression suite [`scripts/todo-graph/tests/test_build.sh`](../../scripts/todo-graph/tests/test_build.sh) gained 8 new sub-tests covering: (a) stamped_items cache shape (file + paired symbol), (b) lint stub-fail on synthetic stub, (c) INTENTIONAL-STUB suppression, (d) real fn no-FP, (e) hand-authored frontmatter forbidden-field, plus three Codex-finding regressions: (f) prototype-before-definition resolves to real def, (g) repo-escape ref refused, (h) corrupt cache yields nonzero rc. 113/113 sub-tests PASS.
